event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/calculator.ts","content":"import { LoanInputState, LoanCalculationResult, YearlyScheduleItem } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローンの元利均等・元金均等返済をシミュレーション計算するユーティリティ関数\n * \n * @param inputs LoanInputState (借入額、金利%、年数、返済方式、ボーナス加算額)\n * @returns LoanCalculationResult (毎月返済額、総返済額、利息総額、年次スケジュール配列)\n */\nexport const calculateLoan = (inputs: LoanInputState): LoanCalculationResult => {\n  const {\n    principal = 35000000,\n    interestRate = 0.5,\n    years = 35,\n    repaymentType = 'equal-payment',\n    bonusAmount = 0,\n  } = inputs;\n\n  const totalMonths = Math.max(1, Math.round(years * 12));\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate / 100 / 12;\n  const bonusTimes = years * 2;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分\n  // ボーナス払いがある場合、ボーナス加算額から逆算、あるいは借入額からボーナス充当元金を分離する一般的な計算\n  // 日本の一般的な住宅ローン計算方式:\n  // ボーナス返済額(1回あたり) × 回数 で概算する方式と、ボーナス元金分を分ける方式がある。\n  // ここでは指定された bonusAmount (ボーナス月加算額) に応じて、借入元金のうちボーナス返済に割り当てる元金を算出する\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal, bonusAmount * bonusTimes);\n    } else {\n      const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n      // 半年ごとの年金現価係数を用いてボーナス加算額からボーナス元金を逆算\n      const pvBonus = bonusAmount * ((1 - Math.pow(1 + halfYearRate, -bonusTimes)) / halfYearRate);\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal, Math.round(pvBonus));\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, principal - bonusPrincipal);\n\n  let monthlyPayment = 0;\n  let monthlyPaymentMax: number | undefined = undefined;\n  let totalInterest = 0;\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const schedule: YearlyScheduleItem[] = [];\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 元利均等返済 (毎月均等)\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n    } else {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round((monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1));\n    }\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n    const actualBonusPayment = bonusAmount;\n\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      // 毎月分の利息と元金\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      let principalThisMonth = monthlyPayment - interestThisMonth;\n\n      if (month === totalMonths) {\n        principalThisMonth = monthlyBalance;\n      }\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月（6ヶ月ごと: 6, 12, 18...）\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusBalance > 0) {\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        let bonusPrincipalPaid = actualBonusPayment - bonusInterest;\n        if (month === totalMonths || bonusPrincipalPaid > bonusBalance) {\n          bonusPrincipalPaid = bonusBalance;\n        }\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - bonusPrincipalPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += bonusPrincipalPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      // 1年経過時または最終月に年次スケジュールを記録\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済 (元金を等分割し、残高に応じた利息を上乗せ)\n    const baseMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    const baseBonusPrincipal = bonusTimes > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusTimes : 0;\n\n    // 初回支払額 (最大支払額)\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    monthlyPaymentMax = Math.round(baseMonthlyPrincipal + firstMonthInterest);\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      const principalThisMonth = (month === totalMonths) ? monthlyBalance : baseMonthlyPrincipal;\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n        const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        const pBonusPaid = (month === totalMonths) ? bonusBalance : baseBonusPrincipal;\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - pBonusPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += pBonusPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 元金均等の代表monthlyPaymentは初回返済額\n    monthlyPayment = monthlyPaymentMax;\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  }\n\n  const totalPayment = Math.round(principal + totalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(monthlyPayment),\n    monthlyPaymentMax: monthlyPaymentMax ? Math.round(monthlyPaymentMax) : undefined,\n    bonusPayment: Math.round(bonusAmount),\n    totalPayment,\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterest),\n    schedule,\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 日本円の通貨フォーマット（3桁カンマ区切り）\n */\nexport const formatCurrency = (amount: number): string => {\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP').format(Math.round(amount));\n};\n\n/**\n * 万円表記フォーマット（例: 3,500万円）\n */\nexport const formatManYen = (amount: number): string => {\n  const man = amount / 10000;\n  if (man >= 10000) {\n    const oku = Math.floor(man / 10000);\n    const restMan = Math.round(man % 10000);\n    return restMan > 0 ? `${oku}億${formatCurrency(restMan)}万` : `${oku}億`;\n  }\n  return `${formatCurrency(man)}万`;\n};","language":"typescript"}

event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/calculator.ts","content":"import { LoanInputState, LoanCalculationResult, YearlyScheduleItem } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローンの元利均等・元金均等返済をシミュレーション計算するユーティリティ関数\n * \n * @param inputs LoanInputState (借入額、金利%、年数、返済方式、ボーナス加算額)\n * @returns LoanCalculationResult (毎月返済額、総返済額、利息総額、年次スケジュール配列)\n */\nexport const calculateLoan = (inputs: LoanInputState): LoanCalculationResult => {\n  const {\n    principal = 35000000,\n    interestRate = 0.5,\n    years = 35,\n    repaymentType = 'equal-payment',\n    bonusAmount = 0,\n  } = inputs;\n\n  const totalMonths = Math.max(1, Math.round(years * 12));\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate / 100 / 12;\n  const bonusTimes = years * 2;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分\n  // 一般的な住宅ローン実務ルールに準拠し、ボーナス返済元金は借入元金の50%を上限として安全ガード\n  const maxBonusPrincipal = Math.floor(principal * 0.5);\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  let effectiveBonusAmount = bonusAmount;\n\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(maxBonusPrincipal, bonusAmount * bonusTimes);\n    } else {\n      const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n      // 半年ごとの年金現価係数を用いてボーナス加算額からボーナス元金を逆算\n      const pvBonus = bonusAmount * ((1 - Math.pow(1 + halfYearRate, -bonusTimes)) / halfYearRate);\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(maxBonusPrincipal, Math.round(pvBonus));\n      \n      // 元金上限に達した場合、1回あたりのボーナス返済額を元金に合わせて再計算\n      if (pvBonus > maxBonusPrincipal) {\n        effectiveBonusAmount = Math.round(\n          (maxBonusPrincipal * halfYearRate) / (1 - Math.pow(1 + halfYearRate, -bonusTimes))\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, principal - bonusPrincipal);\n\n  let monthlyPayment = 0;\n  let monthlyPaymentMax: number | undefined = undefined;\n  let monthlyPaymentMin: number | undefined = undefined;\n  let totalInterest = 0;\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const schedule: YearlyScheduleItem[] = [];\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 元利均等返済 (毎月均等)\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n    } else {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round((monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1));\n    }\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n    let yearBonusPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      // 毎月分の利息と元金\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      let principalThisMonth = monthlyPayment - interestThisMonth;\n\n      if (month === totalMonths) {\n        principalThisMonth = monthlyBalance;\n      }\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月（6ヶ月ごと: 6, 12, 18...）\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusBalance > 0) {\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        let bonusPrincipalPaid = effectiveBonusAmount - bonusInterest;\n        if (month === totalMonths || bonusPrincipalPaid > bonusBalance) {\n          bonusPrincipalPaid = bonusBalance;\n        }\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - bonusPrincipalPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += bonusPrincipalPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n        yearBonusPaid += bonusPrincipalPaid + bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      // 1年経過時または最終月に年次スケジュールを記録\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n          bonusPaid: Math.round(yearBonusPaid),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n        yearBonusPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済 (元金を等分割し、残高に応じた利息を上乗せ)\n    const baseMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    const baseBonusPrincipal = bonusTimes > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusTimes : 0;\n\n    // 初回支払額 (最大支払額)\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    monthlyPaymentMax = Math.round(baseMonthlyPrincipal + firstMonthInterest);\n\n    // 最終月支払額 (最低支払額: 最終月元金 + 最終月利息)\n    const lastMonthInterest = baseMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    monthlyPaymentMin = Math.round(baseMonthlyPrincipal + lastMonthInterest);\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n    let yearBonusPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      const principalThisMonth = (month === totalMonths) ? monthlyBalance : baseMonthlyPrincipal;\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n        const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        const pBonusPaid = (month === totalMonths) ? bonusBalance : baseBonusPrincipal;\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - pBonusPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += pBonusPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n        yearBonusPaid += pBonusPaid + bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n          bonusPaid: Math.round(yearBonusPaid),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n        yearBonusPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 元金均等の代表monthlyPaymentは初回返済額\n    monthlyPayment = monthlyPaymentMax;\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  }\n\n  const totalPayment = Math.round(principal + totalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(monthlyPayment),\n    monthlyPaymentMax: monthlyPaymentMax ? Math.round(monthlyPaymentMax) : undefined,\n    monthlyPaymentMin: monthlyPaymentMin ? Math.round(monthlyPaymentMin) : undefined,\n    bonusPayment: Math.round(effectiveBonusAmount),\n    totalPayment,\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterest),\n    schedule,\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 日本円の通貨フォーマット（3桁カンマ区切り）\n */\nexport const formatCurrency = (amount: number): string => {\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP').format(Math.round(amount));\n};\n\n/**\n * 万円表記フォーマット（例: 3,500万円, 1億2,000万円, 0円, 1万円未満）\n */\nexport const formatManYen = (amount: number): string => {\n  if (!amount || amount <= 0) {\n    return '0円';\n  }\n  const man = amount / 10000;\n  if (man < 1) {\n    return '1万円未満';\n  }\n  if (man >= 10000) {\n    const oku = Math.floor(man / 10000);\n    const restMan = Math.round(man % 10000);\n    return restMan > 0 ? `${oku}億${formatCurrency(restMan)}万円` : `${oku}億円`;\n  }\n  return `${formatCurrency(Math.round(man))}万円`;\n};","language":"typescript"}

'./components/LoanChart';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  // 初期入力状態（3,500万円・年利0.5%・35年・元利均等・ボーナス0円）\n  const [inputs, setInputs] = useState<LoanInputState>({\n    principal: 35000000,\n    interestRate: className=\"header-subtitle\">\n          借入条件から毎月の返済額・返済総額・利息内訳と残債推移を瞬時にシミュレーション\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* サマリーカード */}\n      <section className=\"summary-card\" {inputs.repaymentType === 'equal-principal' &&\n            result.monthlyPaymentMax &&\n            result.monthlyPaymentMin ? (\n              <>\n                <div className=\"summary-value highlight-value\">\n                  <span>{formatCurrency(result.monthlyPaymentMax)}</span>\n                  <span className=\"summary-unit\">円 〜 </span>\n                  <span>{formatCurrency(result.monthlyPaymentMin)}</span>\n                  <span className=\"summary-unit\">円 / 月</span>\n                </div>\n                <span className=\"form-helper\">\n                  ※初回 {formatCurrency(result.monthlyPaymentMax)}円 → 最終回 {formatCurrency(result.monthlyPaymentMin)}円（順次減額）\n                </span>\n              </>\n            ) : (\n              <>\n                <div className=\"summary-value highlight-value\">\n                  <span>{formatCurrency(result.monthlyPayment)}</span>\n                  <span className=\"summary-unit\">円 / 月</span>\n                </div>\n                <span className=\"form-helper\">※全期間定額（元利均等）</span>\n              </>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"summary-item\">\n            <span className=\"summary-label\">ボーナス月加算額</span>\n            <div className=\"summary-value\">\n              <span>{formatCurrency(result.bonusPayment)}</span>\n              <span className=\"summary-unit\">円 / 回</span>\n            </div>\n            {result.bonusPayment > 0 ? (\n              <span className=\"form-helper\">\n                年2回（加算時: {formatCurrency(result.monthlyPayment + result.bonusPayment)}円）\n              </span>\n            ) : (\n              <span className=\"form-helper\">ボーナス払いなし</span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"summary-item\">\n            <span className=\"summary-label\">総返済額</span>\n            <div className=\"summary-value\">\n              <span>{formatManYen(result.totalPayment)}</span>\n            </div>\n            <span className=\"form-helper\">{formatCurrency(result.totalPayment)}円</span>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"summary-item\">\n            <span className=\"summary-label\">利息総額</span>\n            <div className=\"summary-value warning-value\">\n              <span>{formatManYen(result.totalInterest)}</span>\n            </div>\n            <span className=\"form-helper\">{formatCurrency(result.totalInterest)}円</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      {/* メインレイアウト: 入力フォーム ＆ グラフ表示 */}\n      <div className=\"simulator-layout\">\n        <div>\n          <LoanInputForm inputs={inputs} onChange={setInputs} />\n        </div>\n\n        <div>\n          <LoanChart\n            schedule={result.schedule}\n            principal={inputs.principal}\n            totalInterest={result.totalInterest}\n          />\n\n          {/* 年次スケジュール詳細テーブル */}\n          <div className=\"table-card\">\n            <div className=\"table-header-bar\">\n              <div className=\"table-title-group\">\n                <h2 className=\"table-title\">年次返済スケジュール一覧</h2>\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary\">{inputs.years}年間</span>\n              </div>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"toggle-btn\"\n                onClick={() => setShowSchedule(!showSchedule)}\n                aria-expanded={showSchedule}\n              >\n                <span>{showSchedule ? '閉じる' : '詳細表を表示'}</span>\n                {showSchedule ? <ChevronUp size={16} /> : <ChevronDown size={16} />}\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            {showSchedule && (\n              <>\n                <div className=\"chart-tabs\">\n                  <button\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    className={\n                      filterInterval === 'all'\n                        ? 'chart-tab-btn chart-tab-btn-active'\n                        : 'chart-tab-btn'\n                    }\n                    onClick={() => setFilterInterval('all')}\n                  >\n                    全期間（1年ごと）\n                  </button>\n                  <button\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    className={\n                      filterInterval === '5years'\n                        ? 'chart-tab-btn chart-tab-btn-active'\n                        : 'chart-tab-btn'\n                    }\n                    onClick={() => setFilterInterval('5years')}\n                  >\n                    5年刻み表示\n                  </button>\n                  <button\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    className=\"toggle-btn\"\n                    onClick={handleExportCsv}\n                  >\n                    <Download size={14} />\n                    <span>CSV保存</span>\n                  </button>\n                </div>\n\n                <div className=\"schedule-table-wrapper\">\n                  <table className=\"schedule-table\">\n                    <thead className=\"table-header\">\n                      <tr>\n                        <th>年数</th>\n                        <th>年間元金返済</th>\n                        <th>年間利息返済</th>\n                        <th>元金累計</th>\n                        <th>利息累計</th>\n                        <th>ローン残債</th>\n                      </tr>\n                    </thead>\n                    <tbody>\n                      {filteredSchedule.map((item) => (\n                        <tr key={item.year} className=\"table-row\">\n                          <td>{item.year}年目</td>\n                          <td>{formatCurrency(item.principalPaid)}円</td>\n                          <td>{formatCurrency(item.interestPaid)}円</td>\n                          <td>{formatCurrency(item.cumulativePrincipal)}円</td>\n                          <td>{formatCurrency(item.cumulativeInterest)}円</td>\n                          <td className=\"font-semibold\">{formatCurrency(item.balance)}円</td>\n                        </tr>\n                      ))}\n                    </tbody>\n                  </table>\n                </div>\n              </>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

var(--shadow-lg);\n}","language":"css"},{"path":"src/types.ts","content":"/**\n * チャート表示タブの種別 ('area': 残債推移, 'pie': 返済内訳)\n */\nexport type ChartTabType = 'area' | 'pie';\n\nexport interface LoanInputState {\n  principal: number; // 借入額 (円)\n  interestRate: number; // 年利 (%)\n  years: number; // 返済年数\n  repaymentType: 'equal-payment' | 'equal-principal'; // 元利均等 | 元金均等\n  bonusAmount: number; // ボーナス1回あたりの加算額 (円, 年2回)\n}\n\nexport interface YearlyScheduleItem {\n  year: number; // 経過年\n  balance: number; // 残債 (円)\n  principalPaid: number; // 年間元金返済額 (円)\n  interestPaid: number; // 年間利息返済額 (円)\n  cumulativePrincipal: number; // 元金返済累計 (円)\n  cumulativeInterest: number; // 利息返済累計 (円)\n  bonusPaid?: number; // 年間ボーナス返済額 (円, 元金＋利息)\n}\n\nexport interface LoanCalculationResult {\n  monthlyPayment: number; // 毎月の返済額 (元金均等の場合は初回または平均、元利均等なら一定)\n  monthlyPaymentMax?: number; // 元金均等返済時の初回最高支払額\n  monthlyPaymentMin?: number; // 元金均等返済時の最終回支払額 (最低額)\n  bonusPayment: number; // ボーナス月返済加算額 (円)\n  totalPayment: number; // 総返済額 (円)\n  totalInterest: number; // 利息総額 (円)\n  schedule: YearlyScheduleItem[]; // 年次返済スケジュール一覧\n}\n\nexport interface LoanInputFormProps {\n  inputs: 住宅ローンの元利均等・元金均等返済をシミュレーション計算するユーティリティ関数\n * \n * @param inputs LoanInputState (借入額、金利%、年数、返済方式、ボーナス加算額)\n * @returns LoanCalculationResult (毎月返済額、総返済額、利息総額、年次スケジュール配列)\n */\nexport const calculateLoan = (inputs: LoanInputState): LoanCalculationResult => {\n  const {\n    principal = 35000000,\n    interestRate = 0.5,\n    years = 35,\n    repaymentType = 'equal-payment',\n    bonusAmount = 0,\n  } = inputs;\n\n  const totalMonths = Math.max(1, Math.round(years * 12));\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate / 100 / 12;\n  const bonusTimes = years * 2;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分\n  // 一般的な住宅ローン実務ルールに準拠し、ボーナス返済元金は借入元金の50%を上限として安全ガード\n  const maxBonusPrincipal = Math.floor(principal * 0.5);\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  let effectiveBonusAmount = bonusAmount;\n\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(maxBonusPrincipal, bonusAmount * bonusTimes);\n    } else {\n      const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n      // 半年ごとの年金現価係数を用いてボーナス加算額からボーナス元金を逆算\n      const pvBonus = bonusAmount * ((1 - Math.pow(1 + halfYearRate, -bonusTimes)) / halfYearRate);\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(maxBonusPrincipal, Math.round(pvBonus));\n      \n      // 元金上限に達した場合、1回あたりのボーナス返済額を元金に合わせて再計算\n      if (pvBonus > maxBonusPrincipal) {\n        effectiveBonusAmount = Math.round(\n          (maxBonusPrincipal * halfYearRate) / (1 - Math.pow(1 + halfYearRate, -bonusTimes))\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, principal - bonusPrincipal);\n\n  let monthlyPayment = 0;\n  let monthlyPaymentMax: number | undefined = undefined;\n  let monthlyPaymentMin: number | undefined = undefined;\n  let totalInterest = 0;\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const schedule: YearlyScheduleItem[] = [];\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 元利均等返済 (毎月均等)\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n    } else {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      monthlyPayment = Math.round((monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1));\n    }\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n    let yearBonusPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      // 毎月分の利息と元金\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      let principalThisMonth = monthlyPayment - interestThisMonth;\n\n      if (month === totalMonths) {\n        principalThisMonth = monthlyBalance;\n      }\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月（6ヶ月ごと: 6, 12, 18...）\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusBalance > 0) {\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        let bonusPrincipalPaid = effectiveBonusAmount - bonusInterest;\n        if (month === totalMonths || bonusPrincipalPaid > bonusBalance) {\n          bonusPrincipalPaid = bonusBalance;\n        }\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - bonusPrincipalPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += bonusPrincipalPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n        yearBonusPaid += bonusPrincipalPaid + bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      // 1年経過時または最終月に年次スケジュールを記録\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n          bonusPaid: Math.round(yearBonusPaid),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n        yearBonusPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済 (元金を等分割し、残高に応じた利息を上乗せ)\n    const baseMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    const baseBonusPrincipal = bonusTimes > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusTimes : 0;\n\n    // 初回支払額 (最大支払額)\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    monthlyPaymentMax = Math.round(baseMonthlyPrincipal + firstMonthInterest);\n\n    // 最終月支払額 (最低支払額: 最終月元金 + 最終月利息)\n    const lastMonthInterest = baseMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    monthlyPaymentMin = Math.round(baseMonthlyPrincipal + lastMonthInterest);\n\n    let monthlyBalance = monthlyPrincipal;\n    let bonusBalance = bonusPrincipal;\n    let yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n    let yearInterestPaid = 0;\n    let yearBonusPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let month = 1; month <= totalMonths; month++) {\n      const interestThisMonth = monthlyBalance * monthlyRate;\n      const principalThisMonth = (month === totalMonths) ? monthlyBalance : baseMonthlyPrincipal;\n\n      monthlyBalance = Math.max(0, monthlyBalance - principalThisMonth);\n      yearPrincipalPaid += principalThisMonth;\n      yearInterestPaid += interestThisMonth;\n\n      // ボーナス月\n      if (month % 6 === 0 && bonusTimes > 0) {\n        const halfYearRate = interestRate / 100 / 2;\n        const bonusInterest = bonusBalance * halfYearRate;\n        const pBonusPaid = (month === totalMonths) ? bonusBalance : baseBonusPrincipal;\n        bonusBalance = Math.max(0, bonusBalance - pBonusPaid);\n        yearPrincipalPaid += pBonusPaid;\n        yearInterestPaid += bonusInterest;\n        yearBonusPaid += pBonusPaid + bonusInterest;\n      }\n\n      if (month % 12 === 0 || month === totalMonths) {\n        const currentYear = Math.ceil(month / 12);\n        cumulativePrincipal += yearPrincipalPaid;\n        cumulativeInterest += yearInterestPaid;\n        currentBalance = Math.max(0, principal - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n        schedule.push({\n          year: currentYear,\n          balance: Math.round(currentBalance),\n          principalPaid: Math.round(yearPrincipalPaid),\n          interestPaid: Math.round(yearInterestPaid),\n          cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal),\n          cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n          bonusPaid: Math.round(yearBonusPaid),\n        });\n\n        yearPrincipalPaid = 0;\n        yearInterestPaid = 0;\n        yearBonusPaid = 0;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 元金均等の代表monthlyPaymentは初回返済額\n    monthlyPayment = monthlyPaymentMax;\n    totalInterest = cumulativeInterest;\n  }\n\n  const totalPayment = Math.round(principal + totalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(monthlyPayment),\n    monthlyPaymentMax: monthlyPaymentMax ? Math.round(monthlyPaymentMax) : undefined,\n    monthlyPaymentMin: monthlyPaymentMin ? Math.round(monthlyPaymentMin) : undefined,\n    bonusPayment: Math.round(effectiveBonusAmount),\n    totalPayment,\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterest),\n    schedule,\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 日本円の通貨フォーマット（3桁カンマ区切り）\n */\nexport const formatCurrency = (amount: number): string => {\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP').format(Math.round(amount));\n};\n\n/**\n * 万円表記フォーマット（例: 3,500万円, 1億2,000万円, 0円, 1万円未満）\n */\nexport const formatManYen = (amount: number): string => {\n  if (!amount || amount <= 0) {\n    return '0円';\n  }\n  const man = amount / 10000;\n  if (man < 1) {\n    return '1万円未満';\n  }\n  if (man >= 10000) {\n    const oku = Math.floor(man / 10000);\n    const restMan = Math.round(man % 10000);\n    return restMan > 0 ? `${oku}億${formatCurrency(restMan)}万円` : `${oku}億円`;\n  }\n  return `${formatCurrency(Math.round(man))}万円`;\n};","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/LoanInputForm.tsx","content":"import { ChangeEvent, FocusEvent, useState, 万円単位・入力文字列の一時保持用ローカルState（全消し時の入力操作性確保）\n  const [principalStr, setPrincipalStr] = useState<string>(\n    String(Math.round(inputs.principal / 10000))\n  );\n  const [interestStr, setInterestStr] = String(Math.round(inputs.bonusAmount / 10000))\n  );\n\n  // 外部からの inputs 変更をローカルStateへ同期\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setPrincipalStr(String(Math.round(inputs.principal / 10000)));\n  }, YAxis,\n  Tooltip,\n  Legend,\n  PieChart,\n  Pie,\n  Cell,\n} from 'recharts';\nimport { LoanChartProps } from '../types';\nimport { formatCurrency, formatManYen } from '../utils/calculator';\n\ntype
